# Netlist grammar

Use local .subckt/.ends, local .param and constant arithmetic + - * / in braces.
Elements are X for approved MOS wrappers or local subcircuits, R and C. No includes, models, sources or analyses.
MOS order is drain gate source body. NFET body connects to VSS; PFET body connects to VIN.
Use VSS instead of global node 0. Names are case-insensitive. Full-line * comments and + continuation are supported.
SKY130 W/L are bare numeric micrometres; nf=1 and integer m=1..32.
ADM45 W/L use SI or SPICE suffixes; integer mult=1..32, implicit nf=1, default ad/as/pd/ps=0.
R/C use SI or suffixes (M is milli, meg is mega). Positive R=1..1e8 ohm, C>=1e-15 F; total C and MOS W*L must satisfy spec.json.

# LDO-QUIET-SKY130

Design a continuous-time analog LDO meeting spec.json across conditions.json and the specified load range.
Create dut.spice with `.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF` and `.ends DUT`.
Choose connectivity, bias, compensation and dimensions yourself. No starting circuit is supplied.
Use only the declared MOS family and positive R/C in devices.md. Read measurement.md.
You have 90 minutes of cumulative model-call time with no native-case limit. Use the client to measure and submit your fixed circuit before the deadline.
No Web search. Use only the provided task information and your own knowledge.
Measurements consume cases even when simulation fails. Submit through the client with a rationale, then end your session.

## Time accounting

The budget counts model calls, including provider queuing and transport. ngspice, Python, filesystem tools and their waits do not consume this budget. Model calls overlapping local tools still count. An already admitted measurement may finish after model-budget exhaustion; new design tools close. Use client status for the latest collected model-time reading.
# LDO-QUIET-SKY130

Design a continuous-time analog LDO meeting spec.json across conditions.json and the specified load range.
Create dut.spice with `.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF` and `.ends DUT`.
Choose connectivity, bias, compensation and dimensions yourself. No starting circuit is supplied.
Use only the declared MOS family and positive R/C in devices.md. Read measurement.md.
You have 90 minutes of cumulative model-call time with no native-case limit. Use the client to measure and submit your fixed circuit before the deadline.
No Web search. Use only the provided task information and your own knowledge.
Measurements consume cases even when simulation fails. Submit through the client with a rationale, then end your session.

## Time accounting

The budget counts model calls, including provider queuing and transport. ngspice, Python, filesystem tools and their waits do not consume this budget. Model calls overlapping local tools still count. An already admitted measurement may finish after model-budget exhaustion; new design tools close. Use client status for the latest collected model-time reading.
# LDO-QUIET-SKY130 measurement contract

Numerical stimuli and limits come from spec.json. Connect the same submitted
DUT to VIN, VOUT, VSS and ideal noiseless VREF. The load is a prescribed ideal
finite-slew current sink; the external capacitor is ideal and bench supplied.
No startup, protection, bandgap, yield or layout checks are implied.

## DC and load changes

Run DC at all three loads and light/heavy, middle/heavy and burst profiles.
Meter all DUT terminals. Input/reference currents enter DUT; output/ground
currents leave. Verify KCL within 1 nA. Overhead is
max(Iin-Iout,0)+max(Iref,0), including feedback and bias consumption.
Compare voltage against the fixed 1.2 V target, never the waveform tail.
Both isolated edge directions and the final falling burst edge have recovery
windows. Recovery is the final interpolated entry into the absolute required
band that remains there until the window ends. Retain overshoot, droop, initial
hold error, profile points and all signed currents.

Maximum sample gap is 1e-08 s. Every required window must be fully
covered with finite increasing data. Unsettled complete data retains null
recovery and an observation lower bound; it is MISS when that bound exceeds the
limit. Missing/truncated/invalid data is measurement failure. Unexecuted cases
are incomplete, never PASS. Inclusive limits are compared without rounding.

## Supply measurements

Line stimulus starts at minimum VIN, rises to maximum, and returns to minimum,
at the group's highest load, process, temperature and capacitance. The starting
VIN label does not add a different line trajectory. PSRR saves native VIN/VOUT
phasors at 1 kHz and 1 MHz and reports 20 log10(abs(VIN/VOUT)). Check the native
regulated OP; zero/nonfinite transfers cannot produce infinite rejection.

PSRR, bidirectional line excursion/recovery and output noise are scored in every
public and independent group. Each nominal group contains 11 native processes:
three DC, three load profiles, two PSRR, two noise and one line profile.
There are 198 public plus 33 independent cases, with no duplicate nominal
characterization stage. Standalone noise_low/noise_high/line/PSRR cost one each.
Line uses the tighter line excursion limit; its initial and recovery bands are
the fixed DC/recovery bands. Repeated endpoint trajectories across starting-VIN
labels are repetitions, not extra coverage.

Output noise integrates native output voltage ASD squared from 10 Hz to 100 kHz
at 50 points/decade. Use a linear-frequency trapezoidal PSD integral and then its
square root. VREF and load are ideal noiseless sources. Keep the spectrum in
V/sqrt(Hz), integrated output in V RMS, and a valid regulated OP. Negative ASD,
missing band endpoints, sparse/nonfinite data and nonregulating OP cannot pass.

## Numerical precision

Independent evaluation uses the captured standard grid for every required case.
Numerical precision comparisons use a separately authorized developer allocation.
A standard-grid PASS is not a per-submission convergence proof.
# Shared client

Write request.json and call `atb-client --request request.json`.
Commands: `atb-client help`, `atb-client status`, `atb-client validate`,
`atb-client estimate --analysis nominal --pvt N`,
`atb-client measure --id m001 --analysis nominal --pvt N`,
`atb-client submit --rationale "Design rationale"`.
Use `--cl` in farads and optional `--seed` only when the task supports it.
The client reads dut.spice once for validate/measure/submit and sends its exact UTF-8 contents.
Measure requires experiment_id, analysis and pvt; cl is optional. Use help for analyses and native case costs.
Use a new experiment_id when changing a circuit or measurement request. Completed identical requests reuse their saved result.
Submit requires rationale. Successful submission freezes that circuit for every independent condition and closes exploration.

# Netlist grammar

Use local .subckt/.ends, local .param and constant arithmetic + - * / in braces.
Elements are X for approved MOS wrappers or local subcircuits, R and C. No includes, models, sources or analyses.
MOS order is drain gate source body. NFET body connects to VSS; PFET body connects to VIN.
Use VSS instead of global node 0. Names are case-insensitive. Full-line * comments and + continuation are supported.
SKY130 W/L are bare numeric micrometres; nf=1 and integer m=1..32.
ADM45 W/L use SI or SPICE suffixes; integer mult=1..32, implicit nf=1, default ad/as/pd/ps=0.
R/C use SI or suffixes (M is milli, meg is mega). Positive R=1..1e8 ohm, C>=1e-15 F; total C and MOS W*L must satisfy spec.json.
Maximum source 65536 bytes, 2048 statements, 32 local subcircuits, depth 12, 2048 expansion visits, 512 expanded elements.
Only used subcircuits are allowed. Validation checks each physical copy and each technology's geometry domain.

Per-polarity domains (micrometres), intersect all tt/ss/ff sections and W<=100, L>=0.15:


n tt bins 180 W 0.36 100.0 L 0.15 100.0
n ss bins 180 W 0.36 100.0 L 0.15 100.0
n ff bins 180 W 0.36 100.0 L 0.15 100.0
p tt bins 108 W 0.42 100.0 L 0.15 100.0
p ss bins 108 W 0.42 100.0 L 0.15 100.0
p ff bins 108 W 0.42 100.0 L 0.15 100.0

MODEL

I’m starting with a PMOS pass device, a differential error amplifier, and a local feedback buffer to drive the pass gate. The buffer should help keep the gate fast at low bias current, while the output capacitor provides the main filtering.

ACTION
0:04:59
Bash
/bin/bash -lc "cat > dut.spice <<'EOF'
* Continuous-time LDO, 1.2 V from an ideal 0.6 V reference.
.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF
* Supply-derived bias and matched current mirrors.

/bin/bash -lc "cat > summarize.py <<'PY'
import json, sys
for filename in sys.argv[1:]:
 x=json.load(open(filename))
 print(filename, 'ok',x.get('ok'),'revision',x.get('revision'),'pvt',x.get('pvt'),'cl',x.get('cl'),'seconds',x.get('budget',{}).get('remaining_seconds'))
 if 'summary' not in x:
  print(x)
  continue
 print('resources',x['summary']['params']['resources'])
 for c in x['cases']:
  m=c.get('measurements',{})
  print(c['case_id'],c['status'], 'V',round(m.get('vout_v',0),7),'Iq_uA',round(1e6*m.get('overhead_a',0),3),end=' ')
  if 'envelope' in m:
   print('envelope_mV',round(1e3*max(m['envelope']['droop_v'],m['envelope']['overshoot_v']),4), 'rec_us',[None if w['recovery_s'] is None else round(1e6*w['recovery_s'],4) for w in m['recovery_windows']],end=' ')
  if 'psrr_db' in m: print('PSRR',m['psrr_db'],end=' ')
  if 'noise' in m: print('noise_uV',1e6*m['noise'].get('output_noise_rms_v',-1),end=' ')
  print()
 for r in x['summary']['spec_table']:
  if r['verdict']"'!='"'PASS':print('FAIL',r['case_id'],r['metric'],r['measured'],r['verdict'])
PY"

MODEL

The first circuit regulates and handles load changes at nominal conditions, but misses the noise limit. Larger amplifier devices bring nominal noise down to about 24 µV RMS; they also cause a high-frequency supply-rejection miss. I’m adjusting compensation to reduce that peaking before testing the corners.

/bin/bash -lc "cp dut.spice trial08.spice
cat > probe.py <<'PY'
import subprocess,json
from pathlib import Path
base=Path('trial08.spice').read_text()
for node in ['EA','PG','ND','ST','DP1','DP2','SB','NB']:
 Path('dut.spice').write_text(base.replace('.ends DUT',f'RPROBE {node} VREF 100meg\\n.ends DUT'))
 for pvt in ['N','SLC','FHH']:
  eid=f'probe_{node.lower()}_{pvt.lower()}'
  req=Path(f'request_{eid}.json')
  req.write_text(json.dumps({'action':'measure','experiment_id':eid,'analysis':'dc','pvt':pvt}))
  r=subprocess.run(['atb-client','--request',str(req)],text=True,capture_output=True)
  Path(f'{eid}.json').write_text(r.stdout)
  try:
   d=json.loads(r.stdout)
   vals=[]
   for c in d['cases']:
    m=c['measurements']
    ir=m['signed_port_currents_a']['vref']
    vals.append(round(.6-(ir-.5e-6)*1e8,6))
   print(node,pvt,vals,flush=True)
  except Exception as e: print(e,r.stderr,flush=True)
Path('dut.spice').write_text(base)
PY
python3 probe.py"
MODEL

The latest sizing restores useful control at the slow corner, but the amplifier still shows supply-dependent DC error. I’m checking its internal bias voltages with temporary high-resistance probes to identify which devices are limiting the gain.

MODEL

The probes show that the buffer’s output range is limiting regulation at the fast corner. I’m replacing that path with a folded amplifier and an NMOS-input gate buffer, which should give the pass gate the voltage range it needs while retaining low bias current.

/bin/bash -lc "cp dut.spice trial12.spice
cat > probe2.py <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
import json, subprocess, concurrent.futures
base=Path('trial12.spice').read_text()
bases={p:json.load(open(f'm{i:03}.json')) for p,i in [('N',27),('SLC',28),('FHH',29)]}
ib={p:x['cases'][0]['measurements']['signed_port_currents_a']['vref'] for p,x in bases.items()}
def measure(node,p):
 eid=f'q_{node.lower()}_{p.lower()}'
 req=Path(f'request_{eid}.json')
 req.write_text(json.dumps({'action':'measure','experiment_id':eid,'analysis':'dc_low','pvt':p}))
 r=subprocess.run(['atb-client','--request',str(req)],text=True,capture_output=True)
 Path(f'{eid}.json').write_text(r.stdout)
 x=json.loads(r.stdout)
 m=x['cases'][0]['measurements']
 v=.6-(m['signed_port_currents_a']['vref']-ib[p])*1e8
 return p,round(v,6),round(m['vout_v'],6),round(m['overhead_a']*1e6,3)
for node in ['EA','PG','BN','BP','NC','D1','PM','U1','U2','PC','SN','BNB']:
 Path('dut.spice').write_text(base.replace('.ends DUT',f'RPROBE {node} VREF 100meg\\n.ends DUT'))
 with concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=3) as ex:
  print(node,list(ex.map(lambda p:measure(node,p),['N','SLC','FHH'])),flush=True)
Path('dut.spice').write_text(base)
PY
python3 probe2.py"

/bin/bash -lc "cat > public_report.py <<'PY'
import json,sys
from pathlib import Path
out=[]
for name in sys.argv[1:]:
 try: x=json.load(open(name))
 except (OSError,ValueError): print(name,'pending'); continue
 if not x.get('ok') or 'summary' not in x: print(name,x);continue
 ms=[c.get('measurements',{}) for c in x['cases']]
 errs=[r for r in x['summary']['spec_table'] if r['verdict']"'!='"'PASS']
 noise=max([m['noise']['output_noise_rms_v']*1e6 for m in ms if 'noise' in m],default=0)
 ps=[m['psrr_db'] for m in ms if 'psrr_db' in m]
 dc=[m for c,m in zip(x['cases'],ms) if c['case_id'].startswith('dc_')]
 print(name,x['pvt'],x['cl'],'PASS' if not errs else 'FAIL','DCmV',round(max([abs(m['vout_v']-1.2)*1e3 for m in dc],default=0),3),'Iqlight',round(dc[0]['overhead_a']*1e6,2) if dc else None,'noise_uV',round(noise,3),'PSRR',[(round(p['1000'],2),round(p['1000000'],2)) for p in ps])
 for e in errs:print(' ',e['case_id'],e['metric'],e['measured'],e['verdict'])
 out.append({'file':name,'revision':x['revision'],'pvt':x['pvt'],'cl':x['cl'],'pass':not errs,'native_cases':len(x['cases']),'failures':errs,'spec_table':x['summary']['spec_table'],'resources':x['summary']['params']['resources']})
Path('public_summary.json').write_text(json.dumps({'groups':out,'native_cases':sum(a['native_cases'] for a in out),'completed_groups':len(out),'all_pass':all(a['pass'] for a in out),'revisions':list(set(a['revision'] for a in out))},indent=2))
PY
atb-client --request request_status.json"

 if not x.get('ok') or 'summary' not in x:failures.append({'file':str(f),'reply':x});continue
 revs.add(x['revision']);hashes.add(x['summary']['params']['source_sha256'])
 rs=x['summary']['spec_table'];bad=[r for r in rs if r.get('verdict')"'!='"'PASS']
 if {c['case_id'] for c in x['cases']} "'!= caseids:bad.append({'"'metric':'case_coverage'})
 for c in x['cases']:
  if c['status']"'!='"'OK':bad.append({'case_id':c['case_id'],'metric':'case_status','status':c['status']})
 failures.extend({'file':str(f),'pvt':x['pvt'],'cl':x['cl'],'check':r} for r in bad)
 groups.append({'file':str(f),'pvt':x['pvt'],'cl':x['cl'],'pass':not bad,'native_cases':len(x['cases'])})
 rows.extend(dict(r,pvt=x['pvt'],cl=x['cl']) for r in rs)
 print(str(f),x['pvt'],x['cl'],'PASS' if not bad else f'FAIL {len(bad)}')
 for r in bad:print(' ',r.get('case_id'),r.get('metric'),r.get('measured'),r.get('verdict'))
coverage={(g['pvt'],g['cl']) for g in groups}
current=hashlib.sha256(Path('dut.spice').read_bytes()).hexdigest()
metrics={}
def extreme(label,predicate,direction='max'):
 vals=[r for r in rows if predicate(r) and isinstance(r.get('measured'),(int,float)) and math.isfinite(r['measured'])]
 if vals:metrics[label]=(max if direction=='max' else min)(vals,key=lambda r:r['measured'])
extreme('dc_error_max',lambda r:r['metric']=='dc_error_v')
extreme('light_overhead_max',lambda r:r['metric']=='overhead_a' and r.get('case_id')=='dc_low')
extreme('overhead_max',lambda r:r['metric']=='overhead_a')
extreme('load_excursion_max',lambda r:r['metric']=='envelope_error_v' and r.get('case_id')"'!='"'line')
extreme('line_excursion_max',lambda r:r['metric']=='envelope_error_v' and r.get('case_id')=='line')
extreme('recovery_max',lambda r:r['metric'].startswith('recovery_'))
extreme('noise_max',lambda r:r['metric']=='output_noise_rms_v')
extreme('psrr_1k_min',lambda r:r['metric']=='psrr_1000_db','min')
extreme('psrr_1m_min',lambda r:r['metric']=='psrr_1000000_db','min')
extreme('vref_abs_max',lambda r:r['metric']=='vref_abs_a')
complete=not pending and coverage==expected and len(revs)==1 and hashes=={current}
report={'complete':complete,'all_pass':complete and not failures,'completed_groups':len(groups),'native_cases':sum(g['native_cases'] for g in groups),'checked_rows':len(rows),'revisions':sorted(revs),'source_sha256':current,'pending':pending,'groups':groups,'failures':failures,'worst_metrics':metrics}
Path('final_verification.json').write_text(json.dumps(report,indent=2))
print('COMPLETE',complete,'ALL_PASS',report['all_pass'],'groups',len(groups),'cases',report['native_cases'],'pending',len(pending))
for k,v in metrics.items():print(k,v['measured'],v['pvt'],v['cl'],v.get('case_id'))
PY"

rationale=('Continuous-time SKY130 LDO with a large PMOS pass transistor, PMOS-input folded error amplifier, cascoded folding mirror, and a locally regulated NMOS pass-gate buffer. The buffer compares against a supply-shifted gate voltage to retain gain at the slow/cold 1.4 V corner. A slightly raised output-cascode bias preserves light-load swing. Long matched current-source devices and large amplifier devices control overhead and noise. The 1:1 output divider sets 1.2 V from the ideal 0.6 V reference; 88 pF feedback lead and a 70 kohm/12 pF supply-referenced compensation branch give fast recovery and supply rejection with 100 nF to 1 uF external capacitance. Explicit capacitance is 100 pF and summed MOS W*L is 10000 um2. The exact frozen source passes all 198 public native cases across all nine PVT conditions and both capacitor endpoints.')

from pathlib import Path
import json
rationale=('Fixed continuous-time SKY130 LDO using a 3200 um PMOS pass transistor, a PMOS-input folded error amplifier with cascoded folding mirror, and a locally regulated NMOS gate buffer. Supply-shifting the buffer comparison by a 100 kohm/300 kohm divider keeps its input pair active at the slow/cold low-VIN heavy-load corner. A slightly raised output-cascode bias preserves light-load swing. Long matched bias devices and large amplifier devices control current and noise. The equal output-divider resistors set 1.2 V from the ideal 0.6 V reference. An 88 pF feedback lead capacitor and a 70 kohm/12 pF supply-referenced compensation branch balance recovery and supply rejection across 100 nF to 1 uF. Explicit capacitance is 100 pF and summed MOS W*L is 10000 um2. The exact final source passed all checks in nine complete public groups (99 native cases), plus targeted FHH light/heavy recovery and SLC/FLC heavy-load PSRR checks.
